Use it for a search across a repository, a log or a dump to characterise, or any read whose output is large or of unknown size. Not for a one-liner whose output is known to be small. ([SkuratovichA/agorch](https://github.com/SkuratovichA/agorch/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md) · ★1)
- **reader**: Reads and summarizes project context for other agents ([supostat/dev-workflow](https://github.com/supostat/dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/reader.md) · ★1)
- **reader**: The realce pane's agent for its Tools mode, started by the pane alone. It reads the project's files and answers; Read is its only tool. ([Th3-W6rst/claude-realce](https://github.com/Th3-W6rst/claude-realce/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md) · ★1)
- **reader**: Understand a component, file, or subsystem without dumping raw source into the caller's context. Use proactively when an agent needs to map existing code before implementing or planning — especially large files. Returns a structured analysis safe for the caller's context window. ([thewolff/agent-system-2.0](https://github.com/thewolff/agent-system-2.0/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/reader.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **reader**: Use after a batch of work has landed, when the record of what this repository has become should be read end to end and reported on. Reads the wiki, the agent scratchpads, the knowledge log, the open questions, and a server log when pointed at one, and returns a short list of evidenced proposals or nothing. Does not edit. ([01000001-01001110/chat-workbench](https://github.com/01000001-01001110/chat-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Use PROACTIVELY on any finished document that has just been written or substantially revised. Reads it as an outsider with no specialist background and no knowledge of the project, and reports every term, mechanism or leap a real reader could not follow. ([5Bills/claude-plugins](https://github.com/5Bills/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: One persona of the autoauthor reader panel — editor, genre reader, writer, or first reader — answering the reader-panel rubric's questions from arc_summary.md alone. Cheaper tier on purpose: four run in parallel and every verdict is verified against the prose before anything is briefed. ([abnormalend/autoauthor](https://github.com/abnormalend/autoauthor/blob/HEAD/plugin/autoauthor/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Read-only bulk reader (fast tier) for a question that spans many files (where X is defined/used, which files do Y), so the main agent does not load them. Returns file:line leads, not judgments. ([albegali/agentic-portal](https://github.com/albegali/agentic-portal/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reader.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **reader**: Read-only reader for every fan-out leg that opens files and reports (audits, censuses, sweeps, "where does X happen", "what would change if Y"). Use it instead of Explore or general-purpose for any repo read, so the reader rules live here once and the brief carries only the file list and the question. Sonnet by default; the lead passes Opus when being wrong is expensive. Its result comes back in its reply and it never spawns. ([Alberta2514640/clutter](https://github.com/Alberta2514640/clutter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads ONE assigned file and returns its MARK code as JSON. A fresh, bounded context per fact, so nothing ever overflows. ([alcherkas/claude](https://github.com/alcherkas/claude/blob/HEAD/examples/context/subagents/structured-return/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Haiku reader that turns bulk text (a big diff, a DACI, a log, a corpus) into a table with locations — lists, never judges, never edits. For orientation reads the chair or a builder would otherwise carry in context; never for the read that gates or edits. ([alexpeta/rc-submarine](https://github.com/alexpeta/rc-submarine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads a lot and returns little - logs, long outputs, several files, a library's code - and answers in 5-10 lines with a fixed format. Use inside a job when a read would bring hundreds of lines into the main thread that are not needed whole. It does not decide or edit. ([andresdeh/claude-workbench](https://github.com/andresdeh/claude-workbench/blob/HEAD/plugins/continuum/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Answers questions from Arios data. Use for a question about accounts, balances, transactions, bills or the state of a payment, when nothing has to be created or changed. It reads Arios and reports what it found. ([arios-co/plugins](https://github.com/arios-co/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/arios/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads a batch of inputs for the secretary pipeline. For each input it reads the view (text and hits), looks at the image pages the view lists, and writes one digest.txt with a summary, topics, the candidate tickets and the decisions on hits. Used only by the secretary pipeline; it never writes to Todoist or to any other file. ([BBoushe/uni-secretary](https://github.com/BBoushe/uni-secretary/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Read-only lookups on Haiku - finds code, reads files or logs, checks a job's status or a repository's state, and answers briefly with paths and line numbers. Never edits. A slim agent (small fixed context, Haiku prices); use it instead of a general-purpose or Explore agent for searching and reading. ([billw2012/claude-devtime](https://github.com/billw2012/claude-devtime/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Fixture role reader for the role-skill pairing test. ([bionic-coding/crux](https://github.com/bionic-coding/crux/blob/HEAD/crux/scripts/tests/fixtures/role_skill_pairing/green/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads code or docs and reports what they do, changing no files. Use to find where something is defined, trace a flow, extract a contract or spec, summarise behaviour, or compare two implementations. Not for judging a change or a diff; use reviewer. Not for editing anything; use writer. ([bothuany/claude-starter](https://github.com/bothuany/claude-starter/blob/HEAD/plugins/starter/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: You are a read-only codebase explorer. You can search for files, read their contents, and grep for patterns -- but you cannot modify anything. ([bottah/agent-bobs](https://github.com/bottah/agent-bobs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
